# E7.0 — Census of the 28 packages outside Core: names, interfaces, accessors, `final`, `SmartObject`, constants and locators

Subtask of Epic E7 (#462), orchestration issue #705. It satisfies the census half of #694; the
tooling half is the rest of the same PR (`tools/strip-file-headers.php`, `tools/api-surface.php
--package`, `scripts/js-api-surface.mjs <entry>`, `tools/rector/e7-*.php`, `tools/census/e7/`).

**Nothing here is approved by merging it.** After the merge the orchestrator posts the proposed
names and lists below on #462, and Phase 1's semantic work waits for the maintainer's approval
comment (#462 §10–§11). Every later E7 PR implements the approved rows as written; a name,
collapse, accessor or open class that is not in an approved table is an escalation (#462 §3 D5,
§15.2).

**Base commit.** `origin/main` at `8fba49c21` (`fix(ui): define the Core shell's user-menu
messages (#693)`). Its PHP is identical to the plan's base `102926459`:
`git diff --stat 102926459 8fba49c21 -- '*.php'` is empty, so every §1 number applies unchanged.

**Harness.** Every number was measured on that tree in the `fb-e2-app:latest` application image,
with `-e TZ=UTC -e PHP_DATE_TIMEZONE=UTC -e XDEBUG_MODE=off`. `vendor/` was installed with
`COMPOSER_MIRROR_PATH_REPOS=1 composer install`: 29 `vendor/fastybird/*` mirrors, all copies
(`find vendor/fastybird -maxdepth 1 -type l` prints nothing). The census scripts check this
themselves: `tools/census/e7/lib.php` refuses to run while any of the 29 mirrors is a symlink or
differs from its `src/` in any file.

**How the code was read.**

- **PHP code**: every first-party PHP file under `src/`, `tests/`, `bin/`, `public/` and
  `migrations/` (3,495 files, 0 parse errors), parsed with the vendored `nikic/php-parser` and its
  `NameResolver`. Every reference is counted by its resolved FQCN, through imports and aliases.
- **Members**: PHP Reflection through the real autoloader: properties, methods, constants,
  constant values, method bodies (`PhpToken::tokenize()`).
- **Compiled DI graph**: `tools/di-snapshot.php`, all 48 containers (47 compile;
  `test/Plugin/CouchDb` does not, as on every `main` since E4).
- **Tool runs**: `phpcs` with the two E7 carve-outs removed (T9), and Rector dry runs of the two
  committed E7 configs (T5, T9).

"How to reproduce" at the end gives every command.

---

## Proposed names and lists for approval

This is what the maintainer approves at the E7.0 checkpoint. The evidence for every row is in
the per-type sections below.

### A. T1 — role names for the 16 `I`-interfaces and 38 `T`-traits

No name is a mechanical prefix drop: each bare name either already exists beside the type
(the same-namespace collisions in T1), is a reserved word (`switch`), or would read as a
different kind of type. Every proposed name is free in its namespace and its package (checked
by `census.php t1`). None is marked OPEN.

The naming rules applied, from `docs/conventions.md` and Core's precedent:

- **An interface that must stay is named for its role.** The 16 state-store contracts are kept
  (K2 + K3: implemented only by the RedisDb bridges, resolved by type). The module's own caching
  facade beside each one is already the `Repository`/`Manager`, so the contract is named for its
  side of the split: it **reads** or it **writes** the state store. `Reader`/`Writer` was chosen
  over `RepositoryBackend`/`ManagerBackend`, `StateStorage`, and `Finder`/`Persister`, which
  either keep the old noun or describe the implementation rather than the role.
- **A trait that is the default implementation of an interface `X` is `HasX`**, as in Core
  (`Documents\Owner` / `HasOwner`, `Persistence\Entities\EntityCreated` / `HasEntityCreated`).
- **Any other trait is named for the capability it gives its user**, as a verb phrase
  (`FindsAccount`, `HydratesAccount`, `LoadsDevices`, `ReadsRegisters`, `MapsDeviceStates`).

### B. T2 — interfaces: collapses, deletions and open cases

230 interfaces are declared in the 28 packages' `src/`. Under K1–K4 (#460 §3.1) and D6:

- **Keep: 213.** K1, K2 or K3, or a package's `Exceptions\Exception` marker with an implementer
  (D6). That includes `Automator\DateTime\Exceptions\Exception`, which §1.4 listed among the 8
  single-implementer interfaces: D6 keeps a marker while it has an implementer.
- **Collapse: 7** — exactly D6's "other 7"; no K3 use was found for any of them (no
  `findByType()`/`getByType()`/`getDefinitionByType()`/`setImplement()`, no NEON reference):

  | Interface | Collapses into |
  |---|---|
  | `Plugin\ApiKey\Entities\Entity` | `Plugin\ApiKey\Entities\Key` (a Doctrine entity: the mapping must not change, P10) |
  | `Bridge\ShellyConnectorHomeKitConnector\Mapping\Characteristics\Characteristic` | `…\Mapping\Characteristics\BasicCharacteristic` |
  | `Bridge\VieraConnectorHomeKitConnector\Mapping\Characteristics\Characteristic` | `…\Mapping\Characteristics\BasicCharacteristic` |
  | `Connector\FbMqtt\Clients\Client` | `Connector\FbMqtt\Clients\FbMqttV1` |
  | `Connector\NsPanel\Servers\Server` | `Connector\NsPanel\Servers\Http` |
  | `Connector\Viera\Clients\Client` | `Connector\Viera\Clients\Television` |
  | `Connector\Zigbee2Mqtt\Clients\Client` | `Connector\Zigbee2Mqtt\Clients\Mqtt` |

- **Delete: 1** — `Bridge\DevicesModuleUiModule\Exceptions\Exception`: no implementer and no
  `catch` site, as D6 expected.
- **OPEN: 6** (escalation X3) — an implementing chain with several concrete classes but a single
  root, so K1 holds only through subclasses of one class (rule R1):
  `Writers\Writer` in NsPanel, Sonoff, Tuya, Viera and Virtual (root: the abstract
  `Writers\Periodic`, concrete `Event` and `Exchange`), and `Module\Triggers\Entities\EntityParams`
  (root: the abstract `Entities\Triggers\Trigger`).
- **ESCALATE: 3** (escalation X4) — no implementer and no K3 of their own:
  `Connector\NsPanel\Servers\ServerFactory` and `Connector\Viera\Clients\ClientFactory` (the
  parents of one generated factory each), and `Connector\Sonoff\Clients\AutoFactory` (a
  generated-factory shape that nothing registers).

| Property | Today |
|---|---|
| `Module\Accounts\Helpers\Password::$salt` | `getSalt()` / `setSalt()` |
| `Connector\HomeKit\Protocol\Accessories\Accessory::$aid` | `getAid()` / `setAid()` |
| `Connector\HomeKit\Protocol\Characteristics\Characteristic::$valid` | `isValid()` / `setValid()` |

| `Connector\HomeKit\Protocol\Services\Service::$hidden` | `isHidden()` / `setHidden()` |
| `Connector\NsPanel\Protocol\Attributes\Attribute::$valid` | `isValid()` / `setValid()` |
| `Connector\NsPanel\Protocol\Devices\ThirdPartyDevice::$online` | `isOnline()` / `setOnline()` |
| `Connector\NsPanel\Protocol\Devices\ThirdPartyDevice::$provisioned` | `isProvisioned()` / `setProvisioned()` |

Excluded: 121 pairs on Doctrine entities and entity traits, and the 4 `API\Messages\Response`
pairs §1.4 suspected: all 4 (`Tuya…\DataPointState::$dps`, `Viera…\DeviceSpecs::$requiresEncryption`,
`Viera…\Session::$id`, `Viera…\Session::$seqNum`) are orisai `MappedObject`s. No fluent setter was
found. Which visibility each property gets (`public`, `public private(set)`, …) follows the
conventions' rule and is decided in the PR that converts it, from who writes it.

### D. T4 — the classes left open: 185

Every other non-final, non-abstract class becomes `final` (855 candidates). A class stays open
only because it is **Doctrine-mapped** (126) or **extended** somewhere in `src/`, `tests/`,
`bin/`, `public/` or `migrations/` (60, by resolved FQCN). The full list, each with its reason
and `file:line`, is the "Left open" table of each type below: Plugin 6, Automator 7, Addon 6,
Module 66, Bridge 20, Connector 80. Three candidates are mocked by their own class name in tests
(`Plugin\RabbitMq\Channels\Channel`, `Plugin\RedisDb\Clients\Client` and
`Plugin\RedisDbCache\Clients\Client`); E2 left three such classes open because PHPStan's tests configuration could not type
the mock (#492), so E7.2 may need to do the same (§15.1 allows it, with a note).

### E. T6 — live `SmartObject` magic: none

- **No `__call` event dispatch.** §1.6's 8 sites all call a declared method
  (`FbMqttV1::onConnect()`, `onMessage()`, the API clients' private `onOpen()`, Zigbee2Mqtt's
  `Discovery`/`Mqtt::onConnect()` and the subscribers' `onMessage()`). See escalation X1.
- **No `@property` served by the packages' own `SmartObject`.** Of the 99 `@property` lines, 96
  document a property every user declares; 3 (`@property Application\UI\Template $template` in
  Devices' `Presenters\T*` traits) are served by Nette's own `Control::getTemplate()` through
  `Nette\ComponentModel\Component`'s `SmartObject`, which E7 does not touch.
- **No magic `isset`/`unset`** and no `$this->x` read or write that only `__get`/`__set` serve.
- **14 subclasses in another package**, none relying on magic: the HomeKit bridges'
  `Protocol\Accessories\*` (3, of `Connector\HomeKit\Protocol\Accessories\Accessory`) and
  `Protocol\Services\*` (11, of `…\Protocol\Services\Service`).

### F. The other tables, for information

- **T5**: 218 `readonly class` candidates: the 193 Rector finds today (exactly §1.5's number)
  and 25 more once T4's classes are `final`.
- **T7**: §1.3's three cross-package constant families (string), plus a fourth whose parent is
  in Core and which is already typed on both sides (`Core\Security\Entities\Policies\Policy::TYPE`
  / `Module\Accounts\Entities\Roles\Role::TYPE`). `Connector\Virtual\Drivers\DriverFactory::DEVICE_TYPE`
  disagrees on its type (escalation X5).
- **T8**: 6 locators, not 5 (escalation X2). `lazy` is needed for the four `Router\Validator`s;
  the two hydrators' `SchemaContainer` lookups are already broken by E5's lazy
  `fbCore.api.schemas.container`.
- **T9**: the per-type targets; the `#[\Override]` column follows the committed config
  (escalation X6).

### 0.1 Where the census disagrees with #462 §1

| # | §1 says | Measured | Which is right, and why |
|---|---|---|---|

The three groups D6 rests on (22 K2, 8 single-local, 1 without an implementer) agree exactly; the difference is only in the factory and K1 groups, which are kept either way. |
| M2 | §1.4: "41 reference lines outside the declaring directories" for the 16 `I`-interfaces | **27** lines, by resolved FQCN (`t1`) and by `git grep` alike. | The measurement. No decision depends on it. |

The 12 extra entity pairs are on entities outside the modules (ApiKey `Key` 5, HomeKit `Clients\Client` 3, DevicesModuleUiModule data sources 3, Automator `PropertyCondition` 1); they are excluded either way. The 4 `API\Messages\Response` pairs are orisai `MappedObject`s, leaving **9** in scope (T3). |
| M4 | §1.5: "306 are extended somewhere", "**608** candidates" | **60** non-final concrete classes are extended, by resolved FQCN; **855** candidates. | The measurement. §1.5 used a short-name `extends` census, which errs towards open: re-run that way on the same index it gives exactly 608 candidates. 247 classes were open only because another class of the same short name is extended somewhere. The resolved census is exact; the short-name list stays available if the maintainer prefers the conservative cut. |

Escalation X1. |

| M8 | §1.6: "99 lines in about 40 files", classification pending | **99** lines: 96 documentation, 3 Nette's own magic, 0 live magic of the packages' trait. | The measurement (T6). |

| M10 | §1.7: "There are **five** classes" | **6**: also `Module\Ui\Router\Validator`, `getByType(Routing\Router)` at `Router/Validator.php:63`. | The measurement; E5's census already listed four module validators (Accounts, Devices, Triggers, Ui). Escalation X2. |
| M11 | §1.3: 1,441 untyped constants in 571 files; 1,135 fixable, 306 by hand | **The same**, per type too, counting the packages' `src/` **and** `tests/`. `src/` alone: 1,303 in 507 files. | Both. Removing a type's carve-out exposes its `tests/` to `make cs` as well, so 1,441 is the right target (T9). |

Everything else in §1 that T1–T9 touch was re-measured and **agrees**: 2,652 headers (2,531
matching, 120 stale, 1 split: `Plugin/ApiKey/src/Entities/TEntity.php`) and 25,233
forbidden-annotation findings, per type; 16 `I`-interfaces (Devices 12, Triggers 4) and 38
`T`-traits per package; the 8 single-local-implementer interfaces and the one without an
implementer; 2,235 classes, 1,051 `final`, 144 `abstract`, 126 Doctrine-mapped; 193 `readonly`
candidates; 360 `SmartObject` files, 69 `Arrays::invoke()` calls; 7 `missingType.checkedException`
entries (Automator 2, Connector 5).

### 0.2 Escalation candidates (#462 §15.2)

Each needs the maintainer's decision; the census records a default where one is obvious.

- **X1 — §1.6's 8 magic dispatch sites do not exist (M6).** D4 item 7 ("first rewrite the magic
  `$this->onX()` calls to `Arrays::invoke()`") and E7.9's "8 magic calls … with one test per path"
  (§4.6, §13 P9) have nothing to act on. *Default:* E7.9 drops the rewrite and the 8 tests; P9's
  "the only additions are the tests the issue names" then names none for this.
- **X2 — a sixth locator (M10).** D1 widened the locator list "to the 5 measured classes".
  `Module\Ui\Router\Validator` is identical to the other three validators. *Default:* E7.6 takes
  it with the other three (constructor injection + `lazy`, T8).
- **X3 — six interfaces whose K1 holds only through one root (T2, OPEN).** Under R1 they are
  collapse candidates; but each `Writers\Writer` is also the return type of the connector's
  `WriterFactory` family, which `findByType()` collects and the connector selects by mode, and in
  the five other connectors (`FbMqtt`, `HomeKit`, `Modbus`, `Shelly`, `Zigbee2Mqtt`) the same
  interface is K1 with two roots. `Triggers\Entities\EntityParams` is the one module whose
  `EntityParams` has a single root; Accounts', Devices' and Ui's are K1. *Suggested:* keep all
  six, for symmetry with their siblings; or collapse them under R1, as E5 did for Core's `IRouter`.
- **X4 — three factory interfaces with no K reason of their own (T2, ESCALATE).**
  `NsPanel\Servers\ServerFactory` and `Viera\Clients\ClientFactory` each have exactly one generated
  sub-factory (`HttpFactory`, `TelevisionFactory`) and are injected by type into the connector:
  collapse into the sub-factory, or keep. `Sonoff\Clients\AutoFactory` is not registered with
  `setImplement()`, so the `Clients\Auto` client it would create can never be built through it — a
  pre-existing defect or dead code, not an E7 rename.
- **X5 — `Connector\Virtual\Drivers\DriverFactory::DEVICE_TYPE` disagrees on its type (T7).** The
  interface declares `public const DEVICE_TYPE = null;`; `Addon\VirtualThermostat\Drivers\ThermostatFactory`
  overrides it with a string. `?string` on the parent and `string` on the child is legal PHP, and
  E7.4 must type the child first (D8). D8 makes a disagreement an escalation.
- **X6 — which `#[\Override]` target E7 adopts (M5).** The committed `tools/rector/e7-override.php`
  enables `add_to_interface_methods`, matching how E2 applied the rule to Core (#492), which is what
  "`#[\Override]` on every genuine override" means in Core today and what D10's
  `checkMissingOverrideMethodAttribute` will demand. That makes the target 2,099 attributes, not
  694. Two consequences for E7.10's guard: Rector's default skips an override of an **empty**
  parent method, which PHPStan does not; and the guard runs in the **tests** configuration too,
  whose 36 missing attributes (defaults) E7 does not otherwise add. The option is one line in the
  config if the maintainer wants the narrower rule.

### 0.3 Decisions made in the tooling

- `tools/strip-file-headers.php` recognises a header by shape only, never by comparing the
  filename. The filename may be split over lines and may carry a leading `class`/`interface`/
  `trait`/`enum` word (the one split header reads ` * trait TEntity` + `.php`). A
  docblock with no forbidden tag in that position is reported but does not fail `--check`.
- `tools/rector/e7-*.php` take the packages from `E7_PACKAGE` (`Plugin`, `Module/Devices`, a
  comma list), defaulting to all 28. A paths argument would have needed a glob inside a docblock.
- `scripts/js-api-surface.mjs` takes the entry as a positional argument or `--entry <path>`,
  resolved against the repository root.
- The census counts implementers transitively, production code only; an implementer that extends
  another implementer is not a second root (R1, as Core's K1–K4 table applies it).

ApiKey, CouchDb, RabbitMq, RedisDb, RedisDbCache. One PR: E7.2 (#696).

| Type | Kind | Users (implementers / using classes) | Referenced outside its directory | Collision of a bare drop | Proposed name | Role, and why this name |

## How to reproduce

Everything runs in the application image. `tools/census/e7/php.sh` mounts the repository
read-only at `/app` and the scratch directory `E7_DIR` (default `/tmp/e7-census`) at `/e7`, where
the parse index is cached.

```bash
# prerequisite (CLAUDE.md traps): fresh, copied mirrors
docker run --rm -v "$PWD":/app -w /app -e XDEBUG_MODE=off -e COMPOSER_MIRROR_PATH_REPOS=1 \
    fb-e2-app:latest sh -lc 'rm -rf vendor/fastybird && composer install'
find vendor/fastybird -maxdepth 1 -type l          # prints nothing

# T1-T7: no other input
for t in t1 t2 t3 t4 t5 t6 t7; do tools/census/e7/php.sh tools/census/e7/census.php $t; done

# T8: the compiled containers
docker run --rm -v "$PWD":/app -w /app -e XDEBUG_MODE=off -e TZ=UTC -e PHP_DATE_TIMEZONE=UTC \
    fb-e2-app:latest php tools/di-snapshot.php var/tools/di-snapshot/e7 --jobs 4
tools/census/e7/php.sh tools/census/e7/census.php t8 --snapshot /app/var/tools/di-snapshot/e7

# T9: a phpcs report without the E7 carve-outs, and a Rector dry run of the committed config
tools/census/e7/php.sh tools/census/e7/census.php phpcs-ruleset          # writes $E7_DIR/phpcs-e7.xml
docker run --rm -v "$PWD":/app:ro -v /tmp/e7-census:/e7 -w /app -e XDEBUG_MODE=off \
    -e PHP_INI_SCAN_DIR=:/app/tools/php.d fb-e2-app:latest sh -lc \
    'php -d memory_limit=4G vendor/bin/phpcs --standard=/e7/phpcs-e7.xml \
       --sniffs=SlevomatCodingStandard.Commenting.ForbiddenAnnotations,SlevomatCodingStandard.TypeHints.ClassConstantTypeHint \
       --report=json --report-file=/e7/phpcs-e7.json src/FastyBird/{Addon,Automator,Bridge,Connector,Module,Plugin}'
docker run --rm -v "$PWD":/app -w /app -e XDEBUG_MODE=off fb-e2-app:latest sh -lc \
    'make rector-e5 RECTOR_CONFIG=tools/rector/e7-override.php ARGS="--dry-run --clear-cache" > var/tools/rector-e7-override.txt'
tools/census/e7/php.sh tools/census/e7/census.php t9 --phpcs /e7/phpcs-e7.json --rector /app/var/tools/rector-e7-override.txt

# any table, narrowed to a type or a package
tools/census/e7/php.sh tools/census/e7/census.php t4 --package Plugin
tools/census/e7/php.sh tools/census/e7/census.php t2 --package Module/Devices
```

The M5 variants were measured from scratch copies of `tools/rector/e7-override.php` with the
option changed (and, for the §1.5 figure, `{src,tests}` in the path glob). On a slow Docker host,
Rector's parallel workers can time out (`Child process timed out after 120 seconds`); the
`readonly` figure was measured with `->withParallel(900, 4, 16)` added to a scratch copy, which
changes nothing but the timeout.

| Script | What it reads | Tables |
|---|---|---|
| `php.sh` | runs a script in the application image | all |
| `lib.php` | the 28 packages from their `composer.json`; the mirror check; the parse index (declarations, extends/implements/traits, attributes, properties, constants, `catch`, DI lookups, mocks, `$this->x()` calls, property fetches, resolved `I…`/`T…` references) | all |
| `census.php` | dispatches a table; `index`; `phpcs-ruleset` | all |
| `names.php` | the proposed T1 role names | T1 |
